desktop-v1.39.9Reasonix Desktop v1.39.9 Stable2026-10-20T14:44:16Z

修复只有思考内容的回复被当作完成、附加大文件让桌面服务退出、手动压缩后无法发送、流中途被截断的工具调用被当作完成、Studio 导入的会话在 1.x 里显示成多余会话、恢复出来的对话重复出现、恢复状态残留、目标模式工作时长一直为 0、! 命令找不到登录 shell 的程序、远程会话徽标，以及设置与模型实际能力不一致的问题。

发布渠道：稳定版 · v1.39.9

English → · 网页版完整更新日志 →

概览

Reasonix v1.39.9 — Reasonix 1.39.9

修复只有思考内容的回复被当作完成、附加大文件让桌面服务退出、手动压缩后无法发送、流中途被截断的工具调用被当作完成、Studio 导入的会话在 1.x 里显示成多余会话、恢复出来的对话重复出现、恢复状态残留、目标模式工作时长一直为 0、! 命令找不到登录 shell 的程序、远程会话徽标，以及设置与模型实际能力不一致的问题。

发布日期：2026-10-20

Desktop 14 · CLI 5

桌面端更新

重点内容

  • [Desktop · CLI] 只有思考内容的回复现在会重试一次，仍无答案时给出明确提示 — 有些模型只输出思考过程、没有答案也没有工具调用，这一轮却显示为已完成，你只会看到一行思考。现在会再请模型给出一次答案，已经取得的工具结果保留；如果仍然没有答案，这一轮会以明确的错误结束（支持英文、简体中文、繁体中文），说明没有产生答案，不再看起来像是完成了，重新打开会话时这条错误依然在。取消和各项上限的行为不变。导出的诊断信息还会增加不含内容的简短回复摘要（结束原因、大小、工具数量），方便反馈这类问题；它们不会进入模型上下文。 (#13966 by @SivanCola)
  • [Desktop] 附加超大文件不再让桌面服务退出 — 附加大文件（比如安装包、压缩包）可能让桌面服务退出，因为整份文件会先被读进内存并编码，之后才检查大小。现在文件选择、粘贴和拖入只要文件有路径，就直接按路径附加，不再复制一份；只以数据形式存在于页面里的文件（部分粘贴和拖入）会流式写入磁盘，不再整份放在内存里，每次上传的安全上限默认为 1 GiB，可以在你自己的 config.toml 里用 [attachments] max_stream_bytes 调整（设为 0 即取消上限），项目配置不能修改它。超过限制的文件会在读取之前被拒绝，提示里会写明文件名和限制。图片仍保持 64 MB 的限制。 (#13543 by @SivanCola, #12418 by @esengine)
  • [Desktop] 手动压缩后可以继续发送，压缩阈值修改后立即生效 — 手动压缩对话之后，下一条消息可能被挡住、无法与模型沟通，现在已修复。在设置里修改压缩阈值会立即作用于正在打开的会话，不需要替换它；修改无关的偏好也不再重启会话的运行环境。已经在运行的子代理保持启动时的阈值，新建的子代理使用新值。会话因其他原因被替换时，你授予的项目之外文件夹的只读访问会原样保留，不会新增任何访问权限。 (#12501 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop] 会话的完整性索引文件丢失后，不再让所有会话打不开 — 每个附件或历史块旁边都有一个很小的完整性索引文件。如果这些索引文件丢失（比如清理之后，或被其他程序删除），而内容本身完好，所有会话都会因为传输错误而打不开。现在第一次读取时会从内容重建索引，并且只有内容和重建出的索引都与会话里记录的校验值一致才会接受；内容损坏时仍然拒绝。 (#14018, #14019 by @esengine)

修复

  • [Desktop] 模型设置与各模型的实际能力一致，并保留你的默认值 — 选择图片理解模型时，现在会接受 Reasonix 已知能读图的模型（内置的或探测到的）；你明确停用的模型，或官方端点有限制的，仍会被拒绝。子代理的推理强度选项来自各模型自己的能力，过期的选择可以清除。你指定的规划模型即使与默认模型相同也会保持可见，选择「跟随对话模型」会清除这项指定。重命名连接或修改其他无关字段，不再重置它的默认模型和默认推理强度。 (#14005 by @SivanCola)
  • [Desktop · CLI] 工具调用在流中途被截断时，不再被当作已完成的回答 — 使用 Responses 协议的模型，如果流在工具调用尚未完成时就结束，或带着格式错误的调用，以前可能被当作正常回答接受：这一轮只留下调用之前的文字，或者相邻的另一个调用被执行。现在这样的流会作为协议错误被拒绝，该次尝试里没有任何东西被执行，也不会存成最终回答。它不会自动重试，你可以自己继续对话。普通的纯文字回答和完整的调用不受影响。 (#12526 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop · CLI] Studio 导入的会话不再在 1.x 里显示成多余的会话 — Studio 2.x 把它导入的会话以 v4-<id>.jsonl 的文件名写进项目的会话文件夹。1.x 之前会把这些文件当作普通会话、用文件名作标题列出来（有报告称多出 111 行），与它本来就有的对话重复。现在 1.x 把文件名以 v4- 开头的文件视为 Studio 的：不再列出，1.x 清理过期锁文件和租约文件时也不会碰它们。 (#13444 by @esengine)
  • [Desktop] 恢复出来的旧对话不再重复出现，也不再在侧栏里变换身份 — 更新之后，或会话列表在全新缓存上重建时，冲突恢复产生的副本可能被当成单独的对话列出来（有报告里 13 个对话变成 25 行）。现在后台检查完成后，已被其父会话覆盖的副本会重新隐藏。恢复出来的对话在内容校验或父会话被删除之后，也在侧栏里保持同一个身份；会话列表重建或退出时，不再等待一个已被替换的任务。不会改写你的任何会话文件。 (#12822 by @esengine, #13594 by @SivanCola)
  • [Desktop] 导入重试成功后，恢复状态不再残留 — 旧会话因工作区冲突导入失败，之后重试（或重放一次已完成的导入）成功时，之前的恢复状态可能一直留着。现在一旦确认这次导入对应的正是那个来源、版本和目标，就会清除它；其他来源的失败仍保持待处理。这并不会找回从未导入过的会话。 (#12503 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop] 目标模式会显示累计的工作时长 — 一个已经自动进行了好几轮的目标，工作时长仍显示 0s。现在会累计在目标上实际工作的时间（暂停期间和应用关闭的时间不算），随会话保存，重启后仍会显示。这个数值不会提供给模型。 (#12502 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop] 以 ! 开头的命令能找到登录 shell 的 PATH 里的程序 — 从程序坞等启动器打开桌面应用时，它不会继承你在 .zshrc 或 .bashrc 里设置的登录 shell 的 PATH，所以用 ! 输入的命令找不到装在那里的工具。现在运行这类命令之前会读取登录 shell 的 PATH，并与继承来的 PATH 合并，保留原有项并去重；如果无法查询登录 shell，就像以前一样使用继承来的 PATH。 (#13927 by @PeterHepta)
  • [Desktop · CLI] 远程会话也会显示未读圆点，切换会话不再出现误报的连接警告 — 远程会话现在和本地会话表现一致：后台的回复完成时会出现未读圆点，读过之后消失。后台会话完成或结束时会刷新会话列表，不论前台那个是否正在运行。普通的会话切换不再留下误报的连接警告，切走的会话迟到的回复也不会覆盖前台的状态，真实的连接失败仍然可见，连接恢复后会自行消除。要获得完整效果，需要同时更新远程的 Serve 和桌面应用；Serve 较旧时，圆点会退回按时间戳判断。 (#11513 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop · CLI] Go 工具链和网络库升级到已发布安全修复的版本 — Go 工具链升级到 1.26.9，golang.org/x/net 升级到 0.60.0（连同它要求的 golang.org/x 各模块），修复了漏洞扫描在这条线上报出的标准库和 x/net 的漏洞；升级之后，扫描没有再报出 Reasonix 可达的漏洞。 (#12951 by @xxoingr)

CLI 端更新

重点内容

  • [Desktop · CLI] 只有思考内容的回复现在会重试一次，仍无答案时给出明确提示 — 有些模型只输出思考过程、没有答案也没有工具调用，这一轮却显示为已完成，你只会看到一行思考。现在会再请模型给出一次答案，已经取得的工具结果保留；如果仍然没有答案，这一轮会以明确的错误结束（支持英文、简体中文、繁体中文），说明没有产生答案，不再看起来像是完成了，重新打开会话时这条错误依然在。取消和各项上限的行为不变。导出的诊断信息还会增加不含内容的简短回复摘要（结束原因、大小、工具数量），方便反馈这类问题；它们不会进入模型上下文。 (#13966 by @SivanCola)

修复

  • [Desktop · CLI] 工具调用在流中途被截断时，不再被当作已完成的回答 — 使用 Responses 协议的模型，如果流在工具调用尚未完成时就结束，或带着格式错误的调用，以前可能被当作正常回答接受：这一轮只留下调用之前的文字，或者相邻的另一个调用被执行。现在这样的流会作为协议错误被拒绝，该次尝试里没有任何东西被执行，也不会存成最终回答。它不会自动重试，你可以自己继续对话。普通的纯文字回答和完整的调用不受影响。 (#12526 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop · CLI] Studio 导入的会话不再在 1.x 里显示成多余的会话 — Studio 2.x 把它导入的会话以 v4-<id>.jsonl 的文件名写进项目的会话文件夹。1.x 之前会把这些文件当作普通会话、用文件名作标题列出来（有报告称多出 111 行），与它本来就有的对话重复。现在 1.x 把文件名以 v4- 开头的文件视为 Studio 的：不再列出，1.x 清理过期锁文件和租约文件时也不会碰它们。 (#13444 by @esengine)
  • [Desktop · CLI] 远程会话也会显示未读圆点，切换会话不再出现误报的连接警告 — 远程会话现在和本地会话表现一致：后台的回复完成时会出现未读圆点，读过之后消失。后台会话完成或结束时会刷新会话列表，不论前台那个是否正在运行。普通的会话切换不再留下误报的连接警告，切走的会话迟到的回复也不会覆盖前台的状态，真实的连接失败仍然可见，连接恢复后会自行消除。要获得完整效果，需要同时更新远程的 Serve 和桌面应用；Serve 较旧时，圆点会退回按时间戳判断。 (#11513 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop · CLI] Go 工具链和网络库升级到已发布安全修复的版本 — Go 工具链升级到 1.26.9，golang.org/x/net 升级到 0.60.0（连同它要求的 golang.org/x 各模块），修复了漏洞扫描在这条线上报出的标准库和 x/net 的漏洞；升级之后，扫描没有再报出 Reasonix 可达的漏洞。 (#12951 by @xxoingr)

升级提醒

本版本无需手动迁移。

风险提示

  • [Desktop] 恢复组在侧栏里的标识可能变化一次 — 修复旧缓存里没有主题标识的恢复对话组的身份时，该组的标识可能变化一次。你的会话文件里显式写下的标识以及文件本身都不会被改动。 (#13594 by @SivanCola)

下载与安装

  • 官网按平台下载
  • 查看完整差异

贡献者

感谢本版本的贡献者：@SivanCola、@esengine、@XTLine、@PeterHepta、@xxoingr

发布渠道：稳定版 · v1.39.9

概览

Reasonix v1.39.9 — Reasonix 1.39.9

修复只有思考内容的回复被当作完成、附加大文件让桌面服务退出、手动压缩后无法发送、流中途被截断的工具调用被当作完成、Studio 导入的会话在 1.x 里显示成多余会话、恢复出来的对话重复出现、恢复状态残留、目标模式工作时长一直为 0、! 命令找不到登录 shell 的程序、远程会话徽标，以及设置与模型实际能力不一致的问题。

发布日期：2026-10-20

Desktop 14 · CLI 5

桌面端更新

重点内容

  • [Desktop · CLI] 只有思考内容的回复现在会重试一次，仍无答案时给出明确提示 — 有些模型只输出思考过程、没有答案也没有工具调用，这一轮却显示为已完成，你只会看到一行思考。现在会再请模型给出一次答案，已经取得的工具结果保留；如果仍然没有答案，这一轮会以明确的错误结束（支持英文、简体中文、繁体中文），说明没有产生答案，不再看起来像是完成了，重新打开会话时这条错误依然在。取消和各项上限的行为不变。导出的诊断信息还会增加不含内容的简短回复摘要（结束原因、大小、工具数量），方便反馈这类问题；它们不会进入模型上下文。 (#13966 by @SivanCola)
  • [Desktop] 附加超大文件不再让桌面服务退出 — 附加大文件（比如安装包、压缩包）可能让桌面服务退出，因为整份文件会先被读进内存并编码，之后才检查大小。现在文件选择、粘贴和拖入只要文件有路径，就直接按路径附加，不再复制一份；只以数据形式存在于页面里的文件（部分粘贴和拖入）会流式写入磁盘，不再整份放在内存里，每次上传的安全上限默认为 1 GiB，可以在你自己的 config.toml 里用 [attachments] max_stream_bytes 调整（设为 0 即取消上限），项目配置不能修改它。超过限制的文件会在读取之前被拒绝，提示里会写明文件名和限制。图片仍保持 64 MB 的限制。 (#13543 by @SivanCola, #12418 by @esengine)
  • [Desktop] 手动压缩后可以继续发送，压缩阈值修改后立即生效 — 手动压缩对话之后，下一条消息可能被挡住、无法与模型沟通，现在已修复。在设置里修改压缩阈值会立即作用于正在打开的会话，不需要替换它；修改无关的偏好也不再重启会话的运行环境。已经在运行的子代理保持启动时的阈值，新建的子代理使用新值。会话因其他原因被替换时，你授予的项目之外文件夹的只读访问会原样保留，不会新增任何访问权限。 (#12501 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop] 会话的完整性索引文件丢失后，不再让所有会话打不开 — 每个附件或历史块旁边都有一个很小的完整性索引文件。如果这些索引文件丢失（比如清理之后，或被其他程序删除），而内容本身完好，所有会话都会因为传输错误而打不开。现在第一次读取时会从内容重建索引，并且只有内容和重建出的索引都与会话里记录的校验值一致才会接受；内容损坏时仍然拒绝。 (#14018, #14019 by @esengine)

修复

  • [Desktop] 模型设置与各模型的实际能力一致，并保留你的默认值 — 选择图片理解模型时，现在会接受 Reasonix 已知能读图的模型（内置的或探测到的）；你明确停用的模型，或官方端点有限制的，仍会被拒绝。子代理的推理强度选项来自各模型自己的能力，过期的选择可以清除。你指定的规划模型即使与默认模型相同也会保持可见，选择「跟随对话模型」会清除这项指定。重命名连接或修改其他无关字段，不再重置它的默认模型和默认推理强度。 (#14005 by @SivanCola)
  • [Desktop · CLI] 工具调用在流中途被截断时，不再被当作已完成的回答 — 使用 Responses 协议的模型，如果流在工具调用尚未完成时就结束，或带着格式错误的调用，以前可能被当作正常回答接受：这一轮只留下调用之前的文字，或者相邻的另一个调用被执行。现在这样的流会作为协议错误被拒绝，该次尝试里没有任何东西被执行，也不会存成最终回答。它不会自动重试，你可以自己继续对话。普通的纯文字回答和完整的调用不受影响。 (#12526 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop · CLI] Studio 导入的会话不再在 1.x 里显示成多余的会话 — Studio 2.x 把它导入的会话以 v4-<id>.jsonl 的文件名写进项目的会话文件夹。1.x 之前会把这些文件当作普通会话、用文件名作标题列出来（有报告称多出 111 行），与它本来就有的对话重复。现在 1.x 把文件名以 v4- 开头的文件视为 Studio 的：不再列出，1.x 清理过期锁文件和租约文件时也不会碰它们。 (#13444 by @esengine)
  • [Desktop] 恢复出来的旧对话不再重复出现，也不再在侧栏里变换身份 — 更新之后，或会话列表在全新缓存上重建时，冲突恢复产生的副本可能被当成单独的对话列出来（有报告里 13 个对话变成 25 行）。现在后台检查完成后，已被其父会话覆盖的副本会重新隐藏。恢复出来的对话在内容校验或父会话被删除之后，也在侧栏里保持同一个身份；会话列表重建或退出时，不再等待一个已被替换的任务。不会改写你的任何会话文件。 (#12822 by @esengine, #13594 by @SivanCola)
  • [Desktop] 导入重试成功后，恢复状态不再残留 — 旧会话因工作区冲突导入失败，之后重试（或重放一次已完成的导入）成功时，之前的恢复状态可能一直留着。现在一旦确认这次导入对应的正是那个来源、版本和目标，就会清除它；其他来源的失败仍保持待处理。这并不会找回从未导入过的会话。 (#12503 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop] 目标模式会显示累计的工作时长 — 一个已经自动进行了好几轮的目标，工作时长仍显示 0s。现在会累计在目标上实际工作的时间（暂停期间和应用关闭的时间不算），随会话保存，重启后仍会显示。这个数值不会提供给模型。 (#12502 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop] 以 ! 开头的命令能找到登录 shell 的 PATH 里的程序 — 从程序坞等启动器打开桌面应用时，它不会继承你在 .zshrc 或 .bashrc 里设置的登录 shell 的 PATH，所以用 ! 输入的命令找不到装在那里的工具。现在运行这类命令之前会读取登录 shell 的 PATH，并与继承来的 PATH 合并，保留原有项并去重；如果无法查询登录 shell，就像以前一样使用继承来的 PATH。 (#13927 by @PeterHepta)
  • [Desktop · CLI] 远程会话也会显示未读圆点，切换会话不再出现误报的连接警告 — 远程会话现在和本地会话表现一致：后台的回复完成时会出现未读圆点，读过之后消失。后台会话完成或结束时会刷新会话列表，不论前台那个是否正在运行。普通的会话切换不再留下误报的连接警告，切走的会话迟到的回复也不会覆盖前台的状态，真实的连接失败仍然可见，连接恢复后会自行消除。要获得完整效果，需要同时更新远程的 Serve 和桌面应用；Serve 较旧时，圆点会退回按时间戳判断。 (#11513 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop · CLI] Go 工具链和网络库升级到已发布安全修复的版本 — Go 工具链升级到 1.26.9，golang.org/x/net 升级到 0.60.0（连同它要求的 golang.org/x 各模块），修复了漏洞扫描在这条线上报出的标准库和 x/net 的漏洞；升级之后，扫描没有再报出 Reasonix 可达的漏洞。 (#12951 by @xxoingr)

CLI 端更新

重点内容

  • [Desktop · CLI] 只有思考内容的回复现在会重试一次，仍无答案时给出明确提示 — 有些模型只输出思考过程、没有答案也没有工具调用，这一轮却显示为已完成，你只会看到一行思考。现在会再请模型给出一次答案，已经取得的工具结果保留；如果仍然没有答案，这一轮会以明确的错误结束（支持英文、简体中文、繁体中文），说明没有产生答案，不再看起来像是完成了，重新打开会话时这条错误依然在。取消和各项上限的行为不变。导出的诊断信息还会增加不含内容的简短回复摘要（结束原因、大小、工具数量），方便反馈这类问题；它们不会进入模型上下文。 (#13966 by @SivanCola)

修复

  • [Desktop · CLI] 工具调用在流中途被截断时，不再被当作已完成的回答 — 使用 Responses 协议的模型，如果流在工具调用尚未完成时就结束，或带着格式错误的调用，以前可能被当作正常回答接受：这一轮只留下调用之前的文字，或者相邻的另一个调用被执行。现在这样的流会作为协议错误被拒绝，该次尝试里没有任何东西被执行，也不会存成最终回答。它不会自动重试，你可以自己继续对话。普通的纯文字回答和完整的调用不受影响。 (#12526 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop · CLI] Studio 导入的会话不再在 1.x 里显示成多余的会话 — Studio 2.x 把它导入的会话以 v4-<id>.jsonl 的文件名写进项目的会话文件夹。1.x 之前会把这些文件当作普通会话、用文件名作标题列出来（有报告称多出 111 行），与它本来就有的对话重复。现在 1.x 把文件名以 v4- 开头的文件视为 Studio 的：不再列出，1.x 清理过期锁文件和租约文件时也不会碰它们。 (#13444 by @esengine)
  • [Desktop · CLI] 远程会话也会显示未读圆点，切换会话不再出现误报的连接警告 — 远程会话现在和本地会话表现一致：后台的回复完成时会出现未读圆点，读过之后消失。后台会话完成或结束时会刷新会话列表，不论前台那个是否正在运行。普通的会话切换不再留下误报的连接警告，切走的会话迟到的回复也不会覆盖前台的状态，真实的连接失败仍然可见，连接恢复后会自行消除。要获得完整效果，需要同时更新远程的 Serve 和桌面应用；Serve 较旧时，圆点会退回按时间戳判断。 (#11513 by @XTLine)
  • [Desktop · CLI] Go 工具链和网络库升级到已发布安全修复的版本 — Go 工具链升级到 1.26.9，golang.org/x/net 升级到 0.60.0（连同它要求的 golang.org/x 各模块），修复了漏洞扫描在这条线上报出的标准库和 x/net 的漏洞；升级之后，扫描没有再报出 Reasonix 可达的漏洞。 (#12951 by @xxoingr)

升级提醒

本版本无需手动迁移。

风险提示

  • [Desktop] 恢复组在侧栏里的标识可能变化一次 — 修复旧缓存里没有主题标识的恢复对话组的身份时，该组的标识可能变化一次。你的会话文件里显式写下的标识以及文件本身都不会被改动。 (#13594 by @SivanCola)

下载与安装

  • 官网按平台下载
  • 查看完整差异

贡献者

感谢本版本的贡献者：@SivanCola、@esengine、@XTLine、@PeterHepta、@xxoingr

v2.46.0Reasonix CLI v2.46.0 Stable2026-10-20T01:03:18Z

CLI archives built from the Studio release studio-v2.46.0.

View release →

studio-v2.46.0Reasonix Studio v2.46.0 Stable2026-10-20T01:00:19Z

本版新增会话级 Agent 预设（默认关闭）、终端排队消息管理、设置里独立的“会话间消息”、项目内 .reasonix/reasonix.toml，长会话占用的内存明显降低，并修复公网远程误报离线、多标签页掉帧、macOS 托盘图标、1.x 会话导入等一批问题。

新增

  • 新增会话级 Agent 预设：人设、工具和模块按会话绑定版本，在人设页管理，输入框上方的状态行里选择；默认不启用，选择器默认隐藏，可在人设页开启。关联 #13613 by @SivanCola。
  • 预设只能调整 MCP 和扩展的超时，不能改写启动参数、地址或凭据；写预设和切换组合需要配置编辑授权；跨项目移动已绑定的会话会被拒绝，原会话保持不变。关联 #13613 by @SivanCola。
  • 终端里排队的消息画在工作行下：↑ 选中修改，Alt+↑/↓ 调序，Enter 保存，连按两次 d 删除，r 重试，空格暂停。关联 #12144。
  • 设置里的“会话间消息”成为独立的一项，可以从已添加的项目里选择放行、一键撤销。关联 #13794。
  • 提问卡片的自由填写框也能“优化提示词”：点星标或按 Ctrl+Shift+E，改写只在你点“采用”后才替换。关联 #13870。
  • 关联电脑到账号时多一个默认勾选的“加入贡献榜”，旁边显示将公开的名字，点“确认”才生效；终端关联后也会提示加入命令。关联 #13142。
  • 发送反馈时附上各进程的内存（只有进程类型和数字），表单里能先看到，方便判断是界面还是内核占得多。关联 #13899。
  • Studio 项目配置支持 .reasonix/reasonix.toml，仅在根目录的 reasonix.toml 不存在时使用，项目权限限制不变。关联 #6007 fixed in #13887 by @lancy69。

变更

  • 长会话占用的内存更少：已完成的长输出在页面里只保留开头和结尾各 4 KB，可点“显示完整输出”取回；命令运行期间只保留最近 64 KB。关联 #13899。
  • 已完成的子代理卡片收起时不再加载它的子调用，展开才加载；120 个子代理的页面节点从约 5 万降到约 5 千。关联 #13899。
  • 技能子代理（explore、locate 等）在子代理列表里除了名字，也显示它收到的任务的第一行。关联 #13458。
  • 模型派任务给尚未决定信任的项目时，对方窗口保持打开并显示信任问题，并告知任务未发送；后台窗格在等你决定信任时，侧栏会话行也会标出来。关联 #13681、#13890。

修复

  • 公网远程时，较大的历史或文件传输超过半分钟会被误报“请检查电脑是否在线”；现在电脑仍在回传就继续等。关联 #13909。
  • 打开两个及以上标签页时，展开或收起左侧会话栏会掉帧；现在隐藏的标签页不再参与重排。关联 #13936 fixed in #13937。
  • macOS 菜单栏里的托盘图标不再是整个彩色应用图标，改为随浅色、深色菜单栏着色的单色图标。关联 #13929 fixed in #13930。
  • 长时间使用后偶发“缺少本次启动的凭据，请重新打开 Studio 后重试”、发送按钮失效；外壳现在会在认证 cookie 被移除时立即补回，并在日志里记下原因。关联 #13932。
  • 恢复旧会话时，1.x 桌面端自己新建的、编号带 desktop- 或 desktop-manual- 前缀的会话不再被跳过；1.x 里已归档的会话迁移后仍是归档。关联 #13920 fixed in #13921、#13880 by @imcreazy。
  • 会话很多时点开“归档”，读取期间不再显示“没有匹配的会话”，改为“正在读取文件夹…”。关联 #13884。
  • 工作区很大（如整个盘根）时，首个工具最多等待 10 秒的工作区快照，超时记为观察不完整，不再一直卡住。关联 #13883。
  • 侧栏会话行的标题不再被过早截断：不再为没显示的按钮留空位，归档按钮只在悬停或聚焦时占位。关联 #13904 fixed in #13908。
  • 显示左侧图标栏时，侧栏底部的“连接与机器”不再重复出现。关联 #13916。
  • 历史索引遇到暂时读不了的会话文件后，会在它恢复可读时重新索引，不再一直显示“正在建立索引”。关联 #13923 by @chuiwenwei。
  • 代码模式的脚本里 read_file 现在返回文件原文，不再把带行号的显示形式写回文件造成污染。关联 #13945 by @SivanCola。
  • 压缩失败（摘要被截断或拒绝）后不再把日期、技能列表、项目说明重复发给模型，提示不再越滚越大。关联 #13949 by @SivanCola。
  • 根目录忽略规则里以转义的 ! 或 # 开头的模式、以及 core.excludesFile 的读取方式，现在和 git 一致。关联 #13907 by @kevin9327、#13906 by @kevin9327。
  • 记忆迁移遇到读不了的条目时会如实报告而不是中途停止；还原归档时先暂存再发布，避免半途失败丢失。关联 #13912 by @chuiwenwei、#13905 by @chuiwenwei。
  • 读取 OpenAI 兼容流里数字形式的错误码；合并工具进度和前后增量，展开游标跟随最新输出。关联 #13881 by @imcreazy、#13895 by @jf200s。

贡献者

感谢本版本的贡献者：@SivanCola、@lancy69、@imcreazy、@chuiwenwei、@kevin9327、@jf200s

<!-- lang:en -->

This release adds session Agent presets (off by default), terminal queue management, a Session messages entry in Settings and .reasonix/reasonix.toml. Long sessions use much less memory, and it fixes false offline reports over the relay, dropped frames with several tabs, the macOS tray icon and 1.x session import.

New

  • Session Agent presets: personas, tools and modules are bound to a session by version, managed on the Persona page. Off by default; the picker stays hidden until you turn it on. Refs #13613 by @SivanCola.
  • Presets may adjust only MCP and extension timeouts, never launch arguments, endpoints or credentials. Writing presets and switching need configuration-edit authority. Refs #13613 by @SivanCola.
  • Moving a conversation bound to a preset into another project is refused, and the original stays unchanged. Refs #13613 by @SivanCola.
  • In the terminal, queued messages show under the working line: Up selects, Alt+Up/Down reorders, Enter saves, d twice deletes, r retries, Space pauses. Refs #12144.
  • Session messages is its own entry in Settings, where projects can be allowed through from those already added or taken back. Refs #13794.
  • The free-text box of a question card can optimise the prompt: click the sparkle or press Ctrl+Shift+E; the rewrite replaces your text only when you take it. Refs #13870.
  • Linking a computer offers a pre-ticked "join the contribution board" with the public name, applied only on Confirm; the terminal prints the join command. Refs #13142.
  • A report now carries each process's memory (only the process type and numbers), shown in the form first, to tell the interface from the kernel. Refs #13899.
  • Studio loads .reasonix/reasonix.toml when the root reasonix.toml is absent, with unchanged project restrictions. Refs #6007 fixed in #13887 by @lancy69.

Changed

  • Long sessions use less memory: a finished call's long output keeps its first and last 4 KB, with "Show full output" to fetch the rest; a running command keeps its last 64 KB. Refs #13899.
  • A closed, finished sub-agent no longer holds its child calls in the page; 120 sub-agents drop from about 50,000 nodes to 5,000. Refs #13899.
  • A skill sub-agent (explore, locate and the like) shows the first line of its task in the sub-agent list beside its name. Refs #13458.
  • Dispatching to a project you have not yet trusted keeps its window open with the trust question and says the task was not sent. Refs #13681.
  • A background pane asking whether to trust its folder is marked in the sidebar like any other waiting one. Refs #13890.

Fixed

  • Over the public relay, a history or file taking over 30 seconds to arrive was reported as "check the computer is online"; it is now waited for. Refs #13909.
  • With two or more tabs open, folding the left session rail dropped frames; hidden tabs no longer take part in that relayout. Refs #13936 fixed in #13937.
  • On macOS the menu-bar tray icon is a monochrome template glyph that follows the light or dark bar, not the full-colour app badge. Refs #13929 fixed in #13930.
  • The occasional "this launch's credential is missing" refusal after long use, with a dead send button: the shell now restores its authentication cookie if it is removed and logs the cause. Refs #13932.
  • Recovering older sessions no longer skips the conversations 1.x Desktop created itself (ids prefixed desktop- or desktop-manual-), and sessions 1.x had archived stay archived. Refs #13920 fixed in #13921, #13880 by @imcreazy.
  • With a long history, Archived no longer says "No matching sessions" while still reading; it says it is reading. Refs #13884.
  • In a very large workspace (a drive root, say) the first tool waits at most 10 s for the workspace snapshot and records the observation as incomplete. Refs #13883.
  • Session titles in the sidebar are no longer cut short: nothing is held back for buttons that are not showing, and the archive button takes room only while hovered or focused. Refs #13904 fixed in #13908.
  • The sidebar's "Connections and machines" button no longer repeats the Remote entry of the icon rail while the rail is shown. Refs #13916.
  • After a session file was briefly unreadable, the history index retries it once it can be read, instead of staying at "still building". Refs #13923 by @chuiwenwei.
  • In code mode, a script's read_file returns the file's text, so a read-edit-write script no longer writes the numbered display back into the file. Refs #13945 by @SivanCola.
  • After a failed compaction (a truncated or refused summary), the date, skills listing and project instructions are no longer sent to the model again, so the prompt stops growing. Refs #13949 by @SivanCola.
  • An escaped leading ! or # in root ignore patterns, and how core.excludesFile is read, now follow git. Refs #13907 by @kevin9327, #13906 by @kevin9327.
  • A memory migration reports an entry it cannot read instead of stopping; archive restores stage before publishing. Refs #13912 by @chuiwenwei, #13905 by @chuiwenwei.
  • A numeric error code in an OpenAI-compatible stream error is read; tool progress merges with the deltas around it, and the reveal cursor follows the latest output. Refs #13881 by @imcreazy, #13895 by @jf200s.

Contributors

Thanks to this release's contributors: @SivanCola, @lancy69, @imcreazy, @chuiwenwei, @kevin9327, @jf200s

macOS 已用 Developer ID 签名并公证，打开时不需要清除隔离属性。Windows 的窗口、内核与安装器都经 Certum 代码签名证书做了 Authenticode 签名。Linux Debian 系从 .deb 安装，Fedora、RHEL、openSUSE 等从 .rpm 安装（sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm）。x86_64 用文件名带 amd64 的包，aarch64（统信 UOS、麒麟等）用带 arm64 的包。也提供 .AppImage（只提供下载，不会自更新；需要 FUSE 2，或用 --appimage-extract-and-run 启动）。

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

nexu-io/open-design ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
open-design-v0.26.0Open Design 0.26.0 — Share, Review, Refine Stable2026-10-20T14:35:23Z

🌟 Codename: Share, Review, Refine

🔗 The work can leave the project—and the feedback can find its way home. Share an interactive design or motion video with the people who need to review it; let them watch, try it, and comment in context. Then choose the feedback that matters, send it to an Agent, and keep the review connected to the next round of making.

🔥 Highlights

  • 🎞️ Motion Design—make the idea move. Motion Design now appears on Home and in Community, with curated helpers and examples. Start from an editable source, create motion for a video brief, and export an MP4 when the work needs to travel. (#8619)
  • 🔗 One link for the team and the client. Copy a work link and send it out: signed-in reviewers can view and try the work in a browser, without installing the desktop app. Motion videos can travel the same way. The shared page shows the work preview and its comments—not the project's AI conversations or code view. (#8408) Thanks @alchemistklk.
  • 💬 Comment on the part that needs changing. Select the place in the work that needs attention and leave a comment such as “make this title stand out” or “change this button color.” Everyone sees the same feedback on the same page, with less screenshot markup and fewer clarification loops. (#8408) Thanks @alchemistklk.
  • 🤖 Bring feedback back to the project—and hand the revision to an Agent. Comments sync into Open Design. Select the feedback to act on, send it to an Agent, then review the result before the next pass. (#8408) Thanks @alchemistklk.
  • 🔄 Update the work, not the link. After a revision, manually refresh the shared content and reviewers can keep using the same URL. They see that a newer version is available and can refresh to view it; end the share whenever the review is done. (#8408) Thanks @alchemistklk.
  • 🧩 Plugins stay attached to the work you send. Applying a plugin in an existing project now preserves its selected snapshot and respects the capabilities you already granted. (#7597) Thanks @lorenzozanee.
  • 🧠 Bring the model you actually use. During BYOK onboarding, enter a custom model instead of being limited to the suggested list—useful for local Ollama, self-hosted endpoints, and newer models. (#6076) Thanks @dpeterson01.
  • 🌀 A stuck chat can find its way back. When a chat log stops following its layout, Open Design recovers scrolling and keeps wheel and keyboard navigation available. (#8251) Thanks @lefarcen.
  • 🖼️ Finished work shows up where you expect it. After a run creates an image or file, Open Design refreshes its snapshot and opens the result by the existing rules—without taking over a preview you selected yourself. (#8744)

📥 Download: Tag open-design-v0.26.0.

Platform Architecture Assets
macOS Apple silicon DMG · SHA-256
macOS Intel DMG · SHA-256
Windows x64 Installer · SHA-256

✨ Added

🎨 Studio, editing and canvas

  • Motion Design adds curated skills, bundled examples, editable sources, and MP4 production for video-oriented work. (#8619)

🔑 Models and plugins

  • BYOK onboarding accepts custom model names alongside the built-in suggestions. (#6076) Thanks @dpeterson01.
  • Existing projects retain the plugin snapshot and granted capabilities chosen for an apply. (#7597) Thanks @lorenzozanee.

🐛 Fixed

🤝 Sharing and collaboration

  • Signed-in reviewers can view and interact with shared work or motion video in the browser; shared pages intentionally keep project AI conversations and code views private. (#8408) Thanks @alchemistklk.
  • Contextual comments sync back to the project, where selected feedback can be sent to an Agent for the next revision. (#8408) Thanks @alchemistklk.
  • A shared work link stays stable across manually refreshed revisions and can be stopped when the review is complete. (#8408) Thanks @alchemistklk.
  • Existing cloud file identities and aliases are recovered before publishing, so resumed or updated shares keep their original links when local mappings are missing. (#8859, #8867) Thanks @alchemistklk.
  • Uploading duplicate filenames in one batch keeps names and contents in submission order. (#8742)

🧠 Agents, runs and Home

  • Home keeps a draft blocked when funding confirmation is insufficient or unknown; billing changes refresh the hint without sending that draft for you. (#8688, #8775, #8786)
  • The default AMR model is resolved from the caller's available catalog, avoiding an avoidable first-run failure. (#8519) Thanks @Siri-Ray.
  • Cached offline content now respects revocation and expiry across restarts. (#8676)

🖥️ Desktop and workspace

  • Workspace users can complete the desktop update safety check without an unscoped active-run request blocking restart. (#8462) Thanks @Siri-Ray.
  • A saved empty local file selection survives restore instead of being replaced by a default artifact. (#8745) Thanks @open-design-crew.

🙏 Thanks to everyone who shipped 0.26.0

@alchemistklk · @hihicheems · @Siri-Ray · @dpeterson01 · @lefarcen · @lorenzozanee

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.17CC Switch v4.0.17 Stable2026-10-20T14:27:44Z

CC Switch v4.0.17

本版本修复了 4.0.16 带来的两个问题，并改进了托盘快捷面板。轻量模式又能释放托盘面板占用的内存，Windows 上的 WebView2 进程能正常退出；Codex 进入路由时不会再还回一份早已过期的 ChatGPT 登录、卡在登录页。托盘面板在应用之间悬停时不再跳动，在高缩放和小屏幕的 Windows 上也能完整显示；额度条对齐了，并显示距重置还有多久，剩余余额标成绿色；Windows 上左键点托盘图标恢复为打开主界面。Hermes 的供应商标识和内置供应商重名时，表单会给出提醒。另外修复了 Windows 上退出后路由端口仍被占用、Codex 远程压缩的用量记成 0、同时勾选多个 MCP 服务器时有的丢失、只有地址的 MCP 服务器同步到 Hermes 和 OpenCode 后是空的、TOML 字符串里的弯引号被改掉、数据胶囊的 WebDAV 同步失败、macOS 把手输的 API Key 首字母改成大写、在便携版目录上安装 MSI 后仍被当成便携版等问题。4.0 完整的更新内容请见 v4.0 发布说明。

English → | 日本語版 →

改进

Hermes 的供应商标识和内置供应商重名时给出提醒。Hermes 遇到和内置供应商同名的标识（如 deepseek、openrouter）会改用内置那家，要它自己的环境变量 Key，这张卡的地址和 Key 都不生效；4.0.16 只修了 MiniMax 两个预设（#8538）。现在填的标识是 Hermes 内置供应商的标识时，输入框下方会提醒并建议改成 cc-switch-<标识>；已经添加、标识改不了的卡，会提示用别的标识重新添加。kimi 这类别名不受影响，也不会报。提醒不影响保存。

托盘面板的额度显示。各档额度的标签、进度条和重置时间排成整齐的三列，进度条起止对齐，也更长了；重置时间从具体日期改为「还剩多久」，例如「1天23小时后重置」。按量付费的剩余余额只把金额标成绿色，和供应商卡片一致；余额用完时仍显示警告色。

Windows 上左键点托盘图标恢复为打开主界面（#8572）。右键弹出设置里选的托盘样式（面板或菜单）。macOS 不变，左右键都弹出所选样式。

修复

  • 轻量模式下托盘面板一直占着内存（#8612）。4.0.16 起托盘面板默认开启，它是单独的一个窗口，第一次弹出后只隐藏不关闭，进入轻量模式后 Windows 上的 WebView2 进程也不会退出。现在轻量模式下面板一收起就释放，下次点托盘图标再重新打开。
  • Codex 进入路由后卡在 ChatGPT 登录页（#8573）。4.0.16 起，进入第三方路由时如果 auth.json 里没有登录，会还回最近收起的那份；这份登录可能是几个月前收起的、早已过期，Codex 桌面版拿着它反复刷新失败，每次进出路由又会被还回来。现在已过期的登录不再还回，仍留在暂存里，切回官方卡时照常还回。
  • 托盘面板悬停时来回跳动，Windows 高缩放下显示不全（#8572、#8595，#8549、#8553、#8564）。两栏高度改为以主面板为准，供应商多的列表在右栏里滚动，在应用之间悬停时行不会再挪到鼠标下面，只有一个应用时也不再留出大片空白。Windows 上按显示器的缩放比例换算尺寸，面板会收窄并限制在屏幕可用区域内，底部的「打开主窗口」和「退出」不会再被挤出去；挪到缩放不同的另一块屏幕后，第一次弹出也能正确显示。
  • Windows 上退出 CC Switch 后路由端口仍被占用，下次启动报 os error 10048（#8552）。CC Switch 打开的终端和命令行工具继承了监听端口，CC Switch 退出后端口仍被它们占着，下次启动只能回到直连，直到重启电脑或换端口。现在这些子进程不再继承监听端口。
  • Codex 远程压缩的用量记成 0（#8479）。Codex 0.153 起，远程压缩的用量写在另一种记录里，以前没有读取，这些请求都按 0 计。现在会正常计入；升级前已经扫描过的记录保持原样。
  • 同时勾选多个 MCP 服务器时，有的没写进配置文件（#8580）。几次勾选同时写同一个配置文件时会互相覆盖，界面显示已启用，配置文件里却没有。现在会依次写入。
  • 只有地址的 MCP 服务器同步到 Hermes 和 OpenCode 后是空的（#8579）。在 JSON 模式里粘贴或通过深链接导入、只写了 url 没写 type 的服务器，以前被当成本地命令，同步后 Hermes 里只有 enabled: true，OpenCode 里命令为空，界面却显示同步成功。现在和 Codex 一样，有地址、没有命令的按远程服务器处理。
  • TOML 字符串里的弯引号（含中文引号）被改成直引号（#8559）。为了兼容中文输入法，导入 MCP 和编辑 Codex 配置时会把弯引号转成直引号，以前连字符串里面的也一起转了，把 "don’t" 改成 "don't"，有的合法配置还会被拒绝。现在只转换用作字符串边界的引号，字符串里面的保持原样，用中文输入法直接输入引号仍然可以。
  • 数据胶囊的 WebDAV 同步上传失败，返回 500（#8554）。现在同步清单改用通用的文件类型上传，内容不变，新旧版本可以互相读取。S3 同步不受影响。
  • macOS 上手输的 API Key 首字母被自动改成大写。 显示明文时，输入的 sk-… 可能被系统改成 Sk-… 保存下来，导致认证失败。现在这个输入框关闭了自动大写、自动更正和拼写检查。粘贴的 Key 不受影响。
  • 在便携版目录上安装 MSI 后，仍被当成便携版，只能手动下载更新（#8529，#3132）。现在安装时会删掉目录里残留的便携版标记 portable.ini，安装后可以在应用内更新。卸载时不会删除这个文件。

升级说明

数据库结构没有变化，仍然是 20。

  • Windows 上左键点托盘图标恢复为打开主界面（4.0.16 用面板样式时是弹出面板，菜单样式一直如此）；右键弹出「设置 → 通用 → 托盘样式」里选的样式。
  • Codex 远程压缩的用量只从升级后读到的记录开始计入，升级前已经扫描过的不会补算。

致谢

感谢 @funkpopo（托盘面板的滚动与缩放）、@Chaoqi31（Codex 远程压缩用量）、@chonngyi（在便携版目录上安装 MSI）的贡献。

感谢 @SamPheng、@xinxinxiaoxin、@XinwangTao、@ramonmi、@Jdker22、@Geneslves、@Jinmoli、@nanfenggushi、@fereverlove、@hulkbig、@AngryBigCat、@QC2168 报告问题、提供复现信息和修复思路。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。

View release →

Yeachan-Heo/oh-my-codex ↗ 5 releases · 5 stable
v0.21.26 Stable2026-10-20T12:53:42Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.26

0.21.26 is a patch release for team dispatch state isolation and performance improvements (6 commits, 19 files, +722/−269; PRs #3859, #3862, #3863).

Highlights

  • Team dispatch state isolation (#3862): The team dispatch recovery system now isolates degraded observations from authoritative runtime projections, preventing stale state from contaminating recovery paths:

    1. Fallback request isolation: Fallback requests, retries, and delivery observations are stored in each Team's dispatch directory, preventing TypeScript writers from overwriting shared runtime projections.
    2. Fresh state on retry: The merged dispatch reader supplements authoritative state only with proven local fallback requests or durable finalization receipts, ensuring recovery does not reinject stale requests.
    3. Lifecycle preservation: Retry counts and local reasons are preserved after runtime transitions, while authoritative lifecycle timestamps and foreign-Team isolation remain intact. Authored by @shenyankm.

  • Dispatch identity ledger optimization (#3863): The dispatch identity ledger (dispatch-seen.json) is now loaded once per merge instead of per fallback request, reducing file I/O from O(fallbacks) reads to a single read and O(fallbacks) lookups. This improves team mailbox efficiency, especially with high fallback counts. Authored by @Yeachan-Heo.

Compatibility

  • Team dispatch recovery now uses fresh fallback state instead of stale observations; recovery behavior may differ for edge cases involving runtime bridge failures and local fallbacks.
  • Existing dispatch records and team delivery state remain compatible.
  • The team delivery state contract has been updated to reflect the new state isolation semantics.

Validation

  • Pinned candidate 8910439aa4598f1a4e9819b45e11934b0116ab06 passed latest-per-name dev CI: 17 checks succeeded and 8 conditional checks were skipped, with no failures.
  • Release branch CI passed with all required checks green.
  • All code review findings addressed.

Merged PR inventory

v0.21.25 Stable2026-10-20T06:12:34Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.25

0.21.25 is a patch release for team mailbox isolation hardening (2 commits, 3 files, +205/−4; PR #3857).

Highlights

  • Team mailbox isolation hardening (#3857): The team delivery system now fails closed on unconfirmed mailbox creation, rejecting runtime state, persistence, timeout, and invalid acknowledgement failures instead of publishing a successful JSON-only fallback:
    1. Fail-closed behavior on creation failure: Runtime rejections, persistence failures, timeouts, and invalid acknowledgements no longer produce a JSON-only mailbox record.
    2. Partial event reconciliation: Partially persisted creation events are reconciled before assigning another ID, preventing duplicate mailbox IDs within a team scope.
    3. Deduplication across refresh races: The same verified projection is used across refresh races to prevent concurrent creation races from producing multiple mailboxes for the same request. Authored by @shenyankm with @gaebal-gajae.

Compatibility

  • Team mailbox creation now fails when runtime confirmation fails; existing integrations must handle creation failures gracefully.
  • Existing delivered mailboxes and team dispatch records remain compatible.
  • The team delivery state contract has been updated to reflect the new fail-closed semantics.

Validation

  • Pinned candidate 9aa3e7066291a47d1427ad274ed47e0511712ee7 passed latest-per-name dev CI: 18 checks succeeded and 7 conditional checks were skipped, with no failures.
  • Release branch CI passed with all required checks green.
  • All code review findings addressed.

Merged PR inventory

PR Author Title
#3857 @shenyankm fix(team): Fail closed on unconfirmed mailbox creation

Contributors

Thanks to @clawdbot, @shenyankm, and @Yeachan-Heo for contributing to this release.

v0.21.24 Stable2026-10-20T03:01:32Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.24

0.21.24 is a patch release for setup plugin-cache repair hardening (9 commits, 5 files, +365/−28; PRs #3851, #3852, #3853, #3854).

Highlights

  • Repair safety hardening (#3851, #3853, #3854): The setup repair command now enforces stricter requirements on plugin cache removal, preventing false-safe recommendations for live-pinned or unverified caches:

    1. Mandatory removalSafe field validation in dry-run mode, failing closed when safety cannot be verified.
    2. removalSafe derived only from verified managed+unpinned cache eligibility.
    3. Idempotent dry-run behavior returning unchanged for identical caches, matching actual repair behavior. Authored by @Yeachan-Heo.

  • Late-pin scenario test coverage (#3849): Comprehensive removalSafe field tests validate hardening logic against edge cases in pinned-cache handling. Authored by @Yeachan-Heo.

  • Session state gitignore (#3852): The .gitignore now excludes gjc runtime session state to reduce noise in untracked-file listings. Authored by @Yeachan-Heo.

Compatibility

  • omx setup --plugin --repair-plugin-cache behavior is unchanged for valid recovery scenarios; only safety validation and edge-case guidance is improved.
  • Existing task records, plugin configurations, and DAG flags remain compatible.

Validation

  • Pinned candidate e5370084006560eb76df4c79aeecf50e4a3f90f0 passed latest-per-name dev CI: 17 checks succeeded and 8 conditional checks were skipped, with no failures.
  • Release branch CI passed with all required checks green.
  • All code review findings addressed.

v0.21.23 Stable2026-10-19T20:04:52Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.23

0.21.23 is a patch release for evidence requirements in generated team tasks and non-directory plugin cache recovery guidance (5 commits, 14 files, +914/−58; PRs #3840, #3846).

Highlights

  • Evidence requirements for generated team tasks (#3840): Generated team tasks now default to requiring evidence that code changes address the intent, unless the task is explicitly marked as analysis, reporting, or read-only. Task decomposition shares intent classification across CLI and DAG planning to preserve analysis context while requiring evidence for implementation requests. Authored by @shenyankm.

  • Non-directory plugin cache recovery guidance (#3846): Extend cache recovery guidance to cover non-directory entries (symlinks and other non-directory types) in the plugin cache alongside existing pinned-snapshot handling. The repair command continues to emit fail-closed guidance when safe recovery options cannot be determined. Authored by @Yeachan-Heo.

Compatibility

  • Team task evidence requirements follow existing completion gates; generated tasks must provide evidence to match manual task contracts.
  • omx setup --plugin --repair-plugin-cache behavior is unchanged for valid recovery scenarios; only guidance for unsafe entries is improved.
  • Existing task records and explicit DAG flags are preserved and remain compatible.

Validation

  • Pinned candidate 9d811e2250cfdb277591b6087bd37a9194319e78 passed latest-per-name dev CI: 17 checks succeeded and 8 conditional checks were skipped, with no failures.
  • Release branch CI passed with all required checks green.
  • All code review findings addressed (PR #3840 has 10 known-gap Codex findings; see Known gaps section).

Known gaps

  • PR #3840 includes 10 unresolved Codex findings regarding edge cases in the implicit natural-language intent heuristic for distinguishing coding tasks from analysis/reporting. A design pass toward a structured intent signal is pending (follow-up #3845, closed pending design review).

volcengine/OpenViking ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.5.1.1 Stable2026-10-20T12:40:03Z

中文

修复 v0.5.1 中后续 session commit 生成的记忆无法检索的问题：第一次 session commit 正常，但继续 add message 后再次 commit，会出现任务显示完成、新增记忆却无法通过 find 或 search 检索的情况。升级后，后续 commit 生成的记忆可以正常建立索引并被检索。(#6331)

English

Fixes a v0.5.1 bug where the first session commit produced searchable memories, but later commits after adding more messages could report completion while newly generated memories were missing from find and search. Memories generated by subsequent commits are now indexed and searchable. (#6331)

Cua Spaces 0.11.0

New

  • Resources at a glance. Home, each computer's page and the menu bar now show processor, memory and disk use.
  • Prompts for This Mac. Cua asks before a second agent connects, and before an agent does something risky.
  • One first run everywhere. Connect your agent, and This Mac is your first Space.
  • Self-hosting now lives under Settings > Advanced.

Better

  • Setup starts with your computer's name already filled in, and every option says in everyday words what it does.
  • The Done screen is honest: it notices the CLI and sign-in you already have.
  • Usage is counted in "remote hours" everywhere you see it.
  • Wording says "computer" instead of "machine".
  • Optional usage data can link to your account, but stays off until our policy is published.

Fixed

  • Sharing a Linux remote desktop asks for the password, says what to do when it can't share, and explains why a share failed.
  • Moving an app session between computers now carries only the items you pick.

cua-driver-rs-v0.37.0cua-driver-rs: v0.37.0 Prerelease2026-10-20T03:41:37Z

Cua Driver 0.37.0

Summary

This release adds 5 features and includes 17 fixes.

Features

  • add browser_select for native HTML select. (#5232)
  • let agents approve Cua Driver's macOS prompts when the owner authorizes it. (#5467)
  • recovery hints on refusals and unverifiable results. (#5493)
  • install the skill pack when setting up an agent, and add get_guide. (#5491)
  • optional brief foreground step for native drag-and-drop on macOS. (#5481)

Fixes

  • list untitled X11 windows the window manager manages. (#5341) Thanks @abonneth.
  • ask for macOS's direct-capture consent during setup, not over the user's window (CUA-1792). (#5352)
  • don't open a background web <select>; steer to set_value (CUA-1795). (#5353)
  • install Antigravity skills in IDE and CLI directories. (#5226) Thanks @Kenmege.
  • verify macOS target focus before dispatching keys. (#5372) Thanks @fynixart.
  • derive the macOS desktop point ratio from the display mode instead of a screenshot. (#5340) Thanks @abonneth.
  • keep rendered controls visible in semantic_v2 and report not_rendered for background tabs. (#5256)
  • deliver background drags in Electron windows through the main process (CUA-1207). (#5351)
  • count a bundle-identifier capture approval as consent (fixes hosted macOS preflight). (#5402)
  • leave focus on a dialog the clicked app opened. (#5258) Thanks @CMGS.
  • uninstall keeps macOS permissions unless asked; release gate pins the app signing requirement. (#5460)
  • report a locked screen and keep the display awake while an agent works. (#5483)
  • match path-style app grants against the full executable path. (#5505)
  • keep the pointer out of macOS window screenshots. (#5480)
  • launch the installed app by path, stop daemons from any copy, drop extra copies. (#5471)
  • let the client wait out the largest tool budget. (#5526) Thanks @abonneth.
  • refuse background menus bound to sibling windows. (#5520) Thanks @saariuslystoned.

Performance

  • read macOS bundle plists in process. (#5369) Thanks @hqhq1025.
  • faster batched steps and window reads, and a nudge to batch. (#5487)

Contributors

Thanks to @abonneth, @CMGS, @fynixart, @hqhq1025, @Kenmege, @saariuslystoned for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

unixwin/niubash ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v1.4.2Niubash v1.4.2 Stable2026-10-20T10:27:59Z

Niubash v1.4.2 packages for Windows, Linux, and macOS.

Engine: rubash git a83a09d32b4a — every platform build in this release compiled that same revision, pinned by scripts/release/rubash-pin.json and fail-hard verified at fetch time (rubash#827). The release compiles the pinned engine, not "whatever rubash master was when the pipeline ran". Verify locally with niu --version, which prints the same revision.

Windows (x64, ARM64): Inno Setup installers (*-setup.exe) add user PATH and Windows Terminal profile integration; portable zips bundle niu with WinuxCmd and bash/sh shims.

Windows packages come with the must-install bundle pre-installed (niubash#189, niubash#209): niugit (native Windows git — the full plugin distro's five entries install through git, so a fresh machine without git.exe no longer fails niu setup), gawk 5.4.1 with plain awk working too, plus ripgrep (rg) and fd — all landing in the bundled WinuxCmd root as wpm JSON-package payloads, assembled at CI time with no runtime downloads, no archive formats beyond the release zip itself, and no symlinks. Pre-install is best-effort, not a promise: on legs where the package index has no matching artifact (currently niugit and gawk on the ARM64 builds; ripgrep and fd have ARM64 artifacts) the package ships WITHOUT that entry — run winuxcmd wpm install <name> once after installing to add it. The pre-install list and its recorded exclusions live in scripts/release/preinstall.json.

niubash-skill-v<version>.zip is the AI agent skill bundle (SKILL.md + references, WinuxCmd-skill layout): unpack it into an agent skills directory, or let the shell do it — niu skill install embeds the same content.

Linux (x86_64, aarch64; glibc): portable tarballs with the niu binary, README, and LICENSE — Unix builds bundle nothing, native system tools are used. Untar anywhere and run ./niu.

macOS (aarch64, x86_64): portable tarballs with the niu binary, README, and LICENSE. Untar anywhere and run ./niu.

Every artifact was smoke-verified on the build OS of the job that produced it, before upload: niu -c 'echo ok', printf 'a\nb\n' | niu -c 'sort | head -1', an offline NIU_PLUGIN_SPEC=<empty> niu plugin sync --bootstrap (exit 0, no output, no network), and a niu --version check against this tag and the engine revision.

Integrity (niubash#311): SHA256SUMS lists the SHA-256 of every shipped artifact, and each Windows installer has a .sha256 sidecar beside it. niu --self-update verifies the installer against the sidecar before running it and aborts rather than execute anything it cannot verify. The same digests feed the winget manifest InstallerSha256.

What's Changed

  • fix(unix): discover the installed shell by platform binary name by @caomengxuan666 in https://github.com/unixwin/niubash/pull/463
  • fix(login): report login_shell shopt as on for argv[0]-marked login shells by @caomengxuan666 in https://github.com/unixwin/niubash/pull/465

Full Changelog: https://github.com/unixwin/niubash/compare/v1.4.1...v1.4.2

View release →

v1.4.1Niubash v1.4.1 Stable2026-10-20T06:17:59Z

Niubash v1.4.1 packages for Windows, Linux, and macOS.

Engine: rubash git 7122186cd317 — every platform build in this release compiled that same revision, pinned by scripts/release/rubash-pin.json and fail-hard verified at fetch time (rubash#827). stablyai/orca ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.232 Stable2026-10-20T06:20:47Z

Thank you so much for using Orca and for your continued support! ❤️

The short version

  • Auto permissions, in chat and terminal: Choose Manual | Auto | Yolo in Settings → Agents. Auto lets supported Claude and Codex agents handle approvals, and new native chats follow your choice too. You can choose a separate permission mode for other agents; existing chats keep their own settings.
  • Native chat (experimental): Open OpenCode 2 directly in chat, find Codex skills by typing $, and pan and zoom image previews. Rewind works in long conversations, and usage limits show when they reset with a Continue button to pick the conversation back up.
  • A better phone composer: Attach files as well as photos, insert skills inline, and choose model, effort and permissions from one sheet. Faster recovery after switching apps and more responsive terminal scrolling make it easier to keep working away from your desk.
  • Smoother SSH work: Managed servers recover from lost pairing over SSH, tunnels follow a restarted server's port, and unsaved editor drafts survive the move to a managed server. File uploads work better over high-latency links, and a configurable Node.js mirror helps on networks that block nodejs.org.
  • More control over your workspace: Space responds faster, lets you inspect changed files before cleanup, and puts row actions in a dedicated menu. Browser pages can ask for a username and password, and Orca asks before a page opens another app.
  • A lighter app and faster Mac updates: Shared desktop and browser assets, fewer duplicate resources and smaller tour animations reduce the app's footprint. macOS updates avoid background installer throttling, helping Orca get back to your work sooner.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

Native chat (experimental): Some chat features require support from the remote host. Keep paired and SSH hosts updated alongside Orca to use the latest capabilities.

Auto permissions: Availability depends on the agent, model and host version. Changing the default applies to new sessions; existing native chats retain their saved permissions.

Product experience

Native chat (experimental)

OpenCode 2 opens in chat, Codex and Pi skills are easier to find, image previews support pan and zoom, and long conversations are easier to resume and rewind. Usage limits explain the pause and offer Continue, while sent images and visuals stay with the chat.

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-10-20T05:24:36Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-1929+g17f43325b1
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1792349434

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

View release →

HyperFrames v0.8.179

Cloud renders on AWS Lambda now deploy straight from the npm install, and each render chunk downloads only its own slice of audio. Old stacks are refused before any upload, and Studio keeps a deselect made while an edit saves.

  • Studio: A deselect made while an edit saves stays deselected (4ff428444, #5907).

Performance

  • Producer: Each distributed chunk downloads only its own slice of the plan audio (52c5fae9a, #5881).

Internal

  • CLI: The late subresource capture test lands its assets well inside the settle cap (7d47194e4, #5913).
  • Repo: The packed consumer check stops failing on npm versions published minutes ago (501b2d29c, #5905).

HyperFrames v0.8.178

Released on 2026-10-19.

Check, snapshot and renders always use the Chrome HyperFrames pinned, and Studio keeps render scratch files out of your project. Installing the CLI skips an unused code formatter, cloud renders move project files once, and colour grades compile faster.

Fixes

  • CLI: Installing the CLI no longer downloads an unused code formatter (b1fe8ddc1, #5906).
  • Check, snapshot and renders use the pinned Chrome, not an older one another tool cached (50e7a59ae, #5900).
  • Studio: Render scratch stays out of the project and error telemetry counts real failures only (93c321285, #5902).
  • CLI: Snapshot --proxy exits once its frames are written, without waiting for copies (35c03a509, #5895).

Performance

  • Cloud: Move project bytes once through shared content storage (992df8b20, #5772).
  • Core: A color grade compiles only the effects it turns on (44bbc5885, #5889).

Docs & Examples

  • Changelog: Weekly digest 2026-10-12-2026-10-19 (563869f0f, #5894).

Internal

  • The file size check fetches two commits, not the whole history, so it finishes in time (68b873f07, #5903).
  • Studio: The edit accuracy bench stops waiting on background lint and thumbnail images (4c798f398, #5899).
  • Engine: Browser tests kill a Chrome whose close hangs instead of timing out (0ce14d644, #5898).

0.13.2 Stable2026-10-19T21:02:37Z

Release Notes

Released on 2026-10-19.

Enhancements

  • Report all installed packages in uv workspace metadata, including packages outside the selected resolution (#22395)
  • Include supported environment restrictions when exporting pylock.toml (#22596)
  • Retry dropped GET and HEAD requests when fetching package and index data (#22672)
  • Preserve encoding declarations in wheel data scripts that require shell launchers (#22526)
  • Show causes and hints when uv python find --script cannot discover an interpreter, including with -q (#22549)
  • Print valid ksh activation instructions and support non-relocatable environments under OpenBSD-derived ksh (#22538, #22572)
  • Allow PEP 508 direct URLs, including Mercurial and extensionless archive URLs, in uv-build's [build-system].requires validation (#22577)

Python

  • Add PyPy 3.11.17 and 3.12.15 (#22578)

Preview features

  • Fetch and parse PEP 825 index-level variant metadata with wheel-variants (#22509, #22511)

Performance

  • Avoid exponential work when simplifying, traversing, and exporting complex dependency markers (#22608, #22615, #22651, #22674)
  • Reduce the time and memory used to parse and store complex dependency markers (#22675, #22676, #22681)
  • Reduce resolver memory use for large dependency graphs, forks, extras, and dependency groups (#22565, #22625)
  • Speed up project discovery in large workspaces by caching member names (#22635)
  • Reduce uv's binary size and speed up CycloneDX exports (#22431)
  • Reduce uv-build binary size, including Linux x86_64 wheels (#22539, #22573)
  • Reduce allocations when parsing TOML (#22679)

Bug fixes

  • Respect --exclude-newer when a newer wheel for the selected source distribution is already cached (#22685)
  • Prevent URL credentials from leaking through diagnostics or persisted metadata (#22600, #22601, #22617, #22622, #22646)
  • Keep separate cache entries for signed --find-links URLs that differ only in sensitive query values (#22630)
  • Honor PEP 751 environment restrictions when installing or syncing from pylock.toml (#22594)
  • Use configured credentials before prompting during uv publish, and avoid prompting when no upload occurs (#22593)
  • Match recursive build globs against complete file paths and ignore invalid license symlinks (#22581, #22583)
  • Return an error for invalid uv pip compile --python-version values instead of panicking (#22727)
  • Reject invalid pylock.toml output filenames before resolution instead of panicking (#22725)
  • Avoid panicking during repeated resolution conflicts involving fixed-priority packages (#22589)
  • Create version aliases correctly when installing managed Python into a relative directory (#22547)
  • Show incompatibilities from failing uv pip check invocations with -q (#22544)
  • Treat logically equivalent dependency markers as equal regardless of construction order (#22680)

Rust API

  • Require the opt-in unsafe-url feature to use raw url::Url APIs in uv-pep508 (#22623)

Documentation

  • Clarify credential lookup, environment synchronization, managed Python, and integration guidance (#22542)

Other changes

  • Use stable sh.astral.* signing identifiers for macOS binaries (#22648)

redhat-et/ripwire ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.6.8 Stable2026-10-19T23:43:34Z

ripwire 0.6.8: more complete first answers, and fewer false edges. This release collects four batches of reviewed changes (trains 28 to 31). --for stops when its answer is complete, --callers and --callees serve more rows whole, --impact of a type finds the code that uses it, and the call graph proves more receivers and hedges more guesses, with the guesses labelled. The numbers below say what was measured, on what, and what each one does not show.

Read this first

  • Caches are rebuilt once. Every ingest cache and quality snapshot an earlier build wrote is refused and re-indexed on first run (parser version 206, cache version 36, snapshot scheme 22).
  • Go: a method's id is now file::Type::method. Quality-baseline rows, .ripwire_quality_acks rows and .ripwire_notes pinned to a Go method stop matching: re-pin them (--notes lists an old note with dangling="1"; --note-add with the same target stores the new id).
  • Scripts that read next=: on a cut --callers answer it is now the next page (the --uses follow-up moved to uses_next=); on a capped --callees page the --expand follow-up moved to expand_next=; on a --for answer with a body fold, <sigs next=> names the first skipped body and the signatures-only call moved to sigs_next=.
  • --for with no --token-budget now stops when it is complete (a 7,500 B target, a 30,000 B guard that says when it fires); an explicit --token-budget is a soft target whose cuts stay recoverable through next=.
  • Git: ripwire now pins repository-configured filter and diff programs to nothing for its own git commands (--doctor reports it). Files behind a repository-local filter (git-crypt, a per-repository LFS driver) are read as stored.
  • --quality-delta=A..B under a memory limit can answer with the kinds it can decide and exit 6 (new) when that partial answer has no gating row; --quality-kinds (new) runs only the kinds you name.

Measured: the same 173 questions, 0.6.5 then 0.6.7 then 0.6.8

Each answer is graded two ways: a stricter method (a rubric item counts only when the answer names the item's primary symbol, or at least two of its listed terms) and a more lenient one (any one listed term). A question counts as answered completely when its first answer covers the question's pre-written must-mention list. Only the first answer to each question counts.

first answer complete, of 173 0.6.5 0.6.7 0.6.8*
stricter method 50 (about 29%) 83 (about 48%) 98 (about 57%)
more lenient method 78 109 127
the 10 questions from one repository we set aside before any build for this release ran (stricter) 2 5 6
answers that claimed a wrong target (mechanical check against pre-listed wrong targets) 3 0 0

* The 0.6.8 column was measured on the train-28 build of 0.6.8, the first of the four batches. The later changes in this release (trains 29 to 31) were measured only on their own target questions, in the next section, and are not in these counts. A re-run on the final 0.6.8 build of the 29 questions the train-28 build had answered completely only by the more lenient method: 9 are now complete by the stricter method too, and none of the 29 lost a matched item (the other 144 questions were not re-run). Grading is mechanical and the same machine ran all three builds. A hand read for false claims covered a sample of the 0.6.7 and 0.6.8 answers; the 0.6.5 answers were barely read, so their false-claim count is a lower bound.

  • Since 0.6.7, 15 questions got better and none got worse (stricter method), and the mechanical wrong-target check found no wrong-target answer in either build.
  • By language (stricter method), 0.6.5 then 0.6.7 then 0.6.8: C 5, 12, 13; C++ 6, 9, 10; JavaScript/TypeScript 13, 20, 23; Python 12, 20, 21; Go 4, 6, 8; Ruby 3, 6, 10; Rust 5, 5, 7; Java 2, 5, 6.
  • What it costs: the first answers to the 60 development questions total 356,443 B with 0.6.7 and 427,247 B with the 0.6.8 build measured here (+19.9%).
  • Other tools: on the 120 of these questions that five other code-intelligence tools also answered (unnamed here; each at its version as of 2026-10-08; one of them covered 108 of the 120), those five answered 13 to 35 completely with the stricter method and 23 to 44 with the more lenient one, against ripwire's 66 and 89 on the same 120 (ripwire at the build described above). On the final 0.6.8 build, 7 of the 23 questions in this set that ripwire answered completely only by the more lenient method are now complete by the stricter one too, and one (fzf-02, in the table below) went the other way; the other 97 were not re-run. A named, complete comparison is planned for 0.7.0.
  • How to read it. On an earlier 163-question round a hand read of the grader's output fell between the two methods (more lenient 99, stricter 75, hand read about 81 to 85), so treat the truth as between them. This is one round on our own questions, and it does not include trains 29 to 31.

Later changes, measured on their own target rows only

Measured per change, on that change's target rows, not a full table. The rows differ per change and overlap, the base builds differ, so do not add these up. "Stricter" and "more lenient" are the two methods above.

batch change rows measured before, then after (items matched, or complete / not)
29 callers served whole up to 50 rows ninja-06 --callers=Stat stricter 2 of 6 items, then 6 of 6 (+270 B)
29 --impact of a type starts from the symbols that name it tmux-10 --impact=grid_cell; hono-10 0 of 32 files, then 32 of 32 (1,341 to 6,141 B); 12 of 14, then 13 of 14
29 minimum arity: an overload a call cannot reach is not listed ninja --callees=ParseDefault the unreachable second candidate is gone; stricter 8 of 8 before and after
29 Go receiver scope (#403); Rust standard-type member gate (#405) fzf --path with qualified names; mdBook --callers=iter "endpoint not found", then a 5-hop chain; 6 false callers removed (stricter 2 of 7 before and after)
30 f.call() and f.apply() are calls fastify-14 incomplete, then complete (both methods)
30 function-local Go/Rust/GDScript declarations are not symbols 28 fzf and nfpm cells; 574 symbols removed on fzf, gotestsum, nfpm stricter 12 before and 12 after: fzf-12 incomplete to complete, fzf-02 complete to incomplete (its old credit rested on a call edge to a function-local var)
30 --for tail rows name their file's top symbols 120 --for cells stricter 41, then 43 (fastify-13, starlette-11); more lenient 88, then 89; none lower; +1.2 KB per answer on average
30 --path hops read Owner.method 16 --path cells none lower; flake8-14 matched 1 of its items, then 4
30 a call's result type types the next receiver (Go, Rust, Java, Kotlin, TypeScript) 8 target cells (nfpm, mdBook); a census on nushell and envoy no cell moved; the binding of 880 call sites on nushell (Rust) and 76 on envoy (Go, Rust, Kotlin) changed, 16 nushell sites checked by hand, 0 false
31 --for appends the bodies of its top ranked rows 53 --for rows stricter +4 (hono-02, hono-11, koa-02, textual-02), none lost; the whole answer grows by 3.4 KB median
31 a callers list is served whole while its rows fit 16,000 B tmux-07 --callers=options_get_number 40 of 121 callers, then 121 of 121, in one call (4,604 to 11,434 B)
31 a qualified annotation proves a class only through an in-tree qualifier 62,750 call sites on six repositories 0 change: an honesty fix that no measured row needed
31 --for's first eight rows name eight files; a .d.ts ranks below its implementation 44 rows stricter 27, then 29 (fastify-11, fastify-13, from the .d.ts rule); more lenient 39 before and after; files named in the first 8 rows 3.96, then 8.00 on average
31 --path names what an unreached path stopped at and offers a hedged bridge; --callees lists closures the 54 hardest rows stricter 1, then 3 (htop-14, nunjucks-21); more lenient 24, then 26; none lower; 0 wrong hits (textual-11 from refused to partial)

What got better, in words

  • Complete answers. --for stops when it is complete, opens one owner body for a "how does X work" question, appends bodies and names more files; --callers and --callees serve up to 50 rows whole; --impact of a type reaches the files that use it; the calls the resolver declined are listed as hedged <dc> rows instead of being counted and dropped; --grep names who reads a constant.
  • Fewer false edges. Go methods carry their receiver type; a local bound to a call takes the callee's declared result type; a Rust call named like a standard collection member binds no in-tree namesake; a TypeScript or Python annotation q.T proves a class only through an in-tree q; x op= v rebinds; Ruby and Python x[k] is a call only where the receiver is proven.
  • Hedged instead of silent. --safe-delete reads risk="hedged" for a method getattr(obj, f"watch_{name}") or this['compile' + x] can reach; a multi-definition answer says which definition each row belongs to.
  • Memory and safety. --quality-delta=A..B holds one commit's index at a time (a 20,356-file repository's HEAD~1..HEAD had been killed at an 8 GiB limit, #387); on Linux the guard counts the cgroup's real charge, not just the resident set.
  • Markdown. A link [text](other.md#slug) edges to that heading instead of the file, and a link no call shares is marked via="link" (@muayyad-alsadi, #409, #414).
  • Languages. Clojure (.clj, .cljs, .cljc, .bb; #349) parsed 4,930 of 4,933 files from four public checkouts; that is parser completion, not call-resolution accuracy. Elixir unquote(...) calls are calls again (#357, #380).
  • Faster. --pr-context=HEAD~30 on this repository: 49.45 s and 45.79 s became 8.66 s and 7.67 s (two interleaved runs each, output byte-identical). The startup git check no longer spawns a git rev-parse (269 to 351 ms in our four-repository measurement).

What did not move, and what it cost

  • Answers are larger: +19.9% on the 60 development questions above; the body fold adds 3.4 KB median to a --for answer; in the callers-band change's sweep, 89 of 158 answers changed, by a median of +3,357 B.
  • One change aimed at five rows and flipped one (rake-02; the bar was four). Two of the three rows built for the owner body flipped (fastify-12, ninja-11); koa-12 did not.
  • C++ and Objective-C call results are not typed yet: a macro can declare a name no reader sees. Rust receivers written as an in-tree type are declined and counted, not bound (5 true mdBook edges declined, 72 false claims removed).

Thanks

Code: @LaimaWu (#380 Elixir unquote, #382 Python imports on shared captures), @mpapis (#396, #401, #412: Ruby mixins and the Rails/DSL work on the shared path; slice 4a, #400), @tonibergholm-codento (#387 report, #391 and #392: the memory fixes) and @tag-und-nacht (#349, Clojure). Ideas and reports: @muayyad-alsadi (#409, markdown as a graph; code in #414, links that bind the exact heading), whose work also inspired the HTML help-wanted issue #417 (slices you can claim). Other help-wanted slices are listed in CONTRIBUTING.md, which now says where Rust, C++ and Go need the most help. Open pull requests we have not merged yet: #397 (@pt-act), #402, #410 and #411 (@lennix1337). Thank you.

Versions and caches

Parser version 206, cache version 36, quality-snapshot scheme 22; the legend dictionary is dictv=30173b0aa2d06473 entries=908. Caches written by 0.6.7 or any earlier build are rebuilt once.

Full notes: CHANGELOG.md, section 0.6.8.

v2.12.0v2.12.0 — New skill: link-building, for earning links and third-party mentions that lift rankings a… Stable2026-10-19T17:05:44Z

New skill: link-building, for earning links and third-party mentions that lift rankings and AI citations.

  • link-building (new, 1.0.0): confirms links are the bottleneck with a page-level link gap before any outreach, then ranks tactics by effort and quality (unlinked mention reclamation, partner and integration pages, moved and broken links, "best X" list inclusion, resource pages, broken link replacement, editorial guest articles) and hands PR, podcasts, directories, reviews, and communities to their own skills. Every placement is judged for SEO authority and for whether AI engines cite the page, so mentions without links count. References cover tactic playbooks, prospecting and qualification with outreach templates and a tracker, and a risk policy built on Google's spam policies (updated 2026-08-28): paid placements carry rel=sponsored or nofollow, plus vendor vetting, manual actions, and when disavow is warranted. Five evals.
  • Routing (patch bumps): seo-audit, content-strategy, directory-submissions, public-relations, competitor-profiling, free-tools, and ai-seo now point link work to link-building. The marketing-loops backlink-prospecting loop runs through it, adds AI-cited pages as prospects, and uses the outreach tracker as its state.
